They\nare the rules `tools/check_plan.py` applies.\n\n## Orders\n\nEach file in `orders/` is one morning. It lists the bakery (`depot`), the\ncustomers to deliver to, and the vehicles available (`fleet`). Positions are\ninteger grid coordinates `x`, `y` in units of 100 m; times are seconds after\nmidnight. The bakery opens at `open` and closes at `close`.\n\nA customer has an integer `id`, a position, a `demand` in crates, a delivery\nwindow `ready`..`due`, and a `service` time in seconds for unloading. Its\n`kind` (cafe, grocer, hotel, market) is for information only and changes no\nrule.\n\nA fleet entry is one kind of vehicle, named by its `type`: `count` vehicles of it are available,\neach carrying up to `capacity` crates. It travels at `pace` seconds per unit of\ndistance, costs `fixed` for being used at all and `per_unit` for every unit of\ndistance it drives, may be out for at most `shift` seconds from leaving the\nbakery to getting back, and, where `range` is not null, may drive at most\n`range` units of distance in the morning. Costs are in cents.\n\n## Distance and time\n\nThe distance between two points is the Euclidean distance between them,\nrounded up to a whole unit. A vehicle takes `distance * pace` seconds to drive\nit.\n\n## A route\n\nA route is one vehicle's morning: it leaves the bakery at its departure time,\nvisits its customers in order, and comes back. Every route visits at least one\ncustomer, and each vehicle runs at most one route.\n\n- The departure is a whole number of seconds, not before the bakery opens.\n- At each customer the vehicle unloads from the later of its arrival and the\n  customer's `ready` time, and that must not be after `due`. Unloading takes the\n  customer's `service` time; the vehicle leaves as soon as it is done.\n- The vehicle must be back at the bakery no later than it closes, and no more\n  than `shift` seconds after its departure.\n- The crates on the route (the sum of its customers' demands) must fit the\n  vehicle's `capacity`, and the distance driven must be within its `range`\n  where it has one. The distance driven is the sum of the rounded legs, from\n  the bakery through each customer in turn and back to the bakery.\n\n## A plan\n\nA plan delivers to every customer of the morning exactly once, uses no more\nvehicles of a kind than the fleet has, and costs the sum over its routes of the\nvehicle's `fixed` cost plus `per_unit` times the route's distance.\n\nIt is written as JSON:\n\n    {\"routes\": [{\"type\": \"van\", \"depart\": 18000, \"stops\": [12, 4, 31]}, ...]}\n\nwhere `type` names a fleet entry, `depart` is the departure time as a JSON\ninteger (`18000`, not `18000.0`) and `stops` lists customer ids, as the\nintegers in the orders file, in visiting order. Other keys are ignored.\n--- targets ---\n{\n \"harbour-lanes\": 77867,\n \"old-town\": 147362,\n \"riverside\": 201921,\n \"north-ring\": 288705\n}--- planner ---\n\"\"\"Current dispatch planner: nearest-next greedy, vans first.\n\nUsage: python3 planner/plan_morning.py orders/<morning>.json plans/<morning>.json\n\nBuilds one route at a time. A route leaves when the bakery opens and keeps\ndriving to the nearest customer it can still reach inside that customer's window\nwith room on board; when nothing fits, it goes home and the next vehicle starts.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\n\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), \"..\", \"tools\"))\nfrom check_plan import distance, evaluate  # noqa: E402\n\nORDER = (\"van\", \"truck\", \"bike\")\n\n\ndef plan(orders):\n    depot = orders[\"depot\"]\n    left = {c[\"id\"]: c for c in orders[\"customers\"]}\n    fleet = sorted(orders[\"fleet\"], key=lambda f: ORDER.index(f[\"type\"]) if f[\"type\"] in ORDER else 99)\n    routes = []\n    for kind in fleet:\n        for _ in range(kind[\"count\"]):\n            if not left:\n                break\n            clock, here, crates, driven, stops = depot[\"open\"], depot, 0, 0, []\n            while True:\n                best = None\n                for c in c[\"demand\"] > kind[\"capacity\"]:\n                        continue\n                    back = start + c[\"service\"] + distance(c, depot) * kind[\"pace\"]\n                    if back > depot[\"close\"] or back - depot[\"open\"] > kind[\"shift\"]:\n                        continue\n                    total = driven + leg + distance(c, depot)\n                    if kind[\"range\"] is not None and total > kind[\"range\"]:\n                        continue\n                    if best is None or leg < best[0]:\n                        best = (leg, c, start)\n                if best is None:\n                    break\n                leg, c, start = best\n                stops.append(c[\"id\"])\n                clock, here, crates, driven = start + c[\"service\"], c, crates + c[\"demand\"], driven + leg\n                del left[c[\"id\"]]\n            if stops:\n                routes.append({\"type\": kind[\"type\"], \"depart\": depot[\"open\"], \"stops\": stops})\n plans/<morning>.json\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport math\nimport sys\n\n\nclass PlanError(ValueError):\n    pass\n\n\ndef distance(a, b):\n    sq = (a[\"x\"] - b[\"x\"]) ** 2 + (a[\"y\"] - b[\"y\"]) ** 2\n    d = math.isqrt(sq)\n    return d if d * d == sq else d + 1\n\n\ndef evaluate(orders, plan):\n    depot = orders[\"depot\"]\n    customers = {c[\"id\"]: c for c in orders[\"customers\"]}\n    fleet = {f[\"type\"]: f for f in orders[\"fleet\"]}\n    used = {t: 0 for t in fleet}\n    served = set()\n    total = 0\n    if not isinstance(plan, dict) or not isinstance(plan.get(\"routes\"), list):\n        raise PlanError(\"a plan is a JSON object with a routes list\")\n    for number, route in enumerate(plan[\"routes\"], start=1):\n        where = f\"route {number}\"\n        if not isinstance(route, dict):\n            raise PlanError(f\"{where}: not a JSON object\")\n        kind = fleet.get(route.get(\"type\")) if isinstance(route.get(\"type\"), str) else None\n        if kind PlanError(f\"{where}: departure must be a whole second, not before opening\")\n        used[route[\"type\"]] += 1\n        clock, here, crates, driven = depart, depot, 0, 0\n        for cid in {cid} reached at {start}, after its window closes at {customer['due']}\")\n            clock = start + customer[\"service\"]\n            crates += customer[\"demand\"]\n            here = customer\n {"type":"item.started","item":{"id":"item_16","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"cat > /tmp/solve.py <<'PY'\nimport json, math, random, sys, time\nfrom functools import lru_cache\n\nclass Solver:\n def __init__(self,path,seed=1):\n  self.data=json.load(open(path)); self.cs=self.data['customers']; self.n=len(self.cs); self.C={c['id']:c for c in self.cs}; self.ids=list(self.C)\n  self.depot=self.data['depot']; self.types={f['type']:f for f in self.data['fleet']}\n  self.slots=[]\n  for f in self.data['fleet']:\n   self.slots += [f['type']]*f['count']\n  pts={0:self.depot}|self.C\n  self.dist={(a,b): self.distance(pts[a],pts[b]) for a in pts for b in pts}\n  self.rng=random.Random(seed)\n  self._eval_cache={}\n def distance(self,a,b):\n  sq=(a['x']-b['x'])**2+(a['y']-b['y'])**2; d=math.isqrt(sq); return d if d*d==sq else d+1\n def ev(self,typ,seq):\n  if not seq:return (0,self.depot['open'])\n  key=(typ,tuple(seq)); z=self._eval_cache.get(key)\n  if z is not None:return z\n  f=self.types[typ]; cap=sum(self.C[i]['demand'] for i in seq)\n  if cap>f['capacity']: z=None\n  else:\n   driven=0; prev=0; a=0; b=-10**18; latest=10**18\n   ok=True\n   for cid in seq:\n    c=self.C[cid]; leg=self.dist[prev,cid]; driven+=leg; a+=leg*f['pace']; b=max(b+leg*f['pace'],c['ready']); latest=min(latest,c['due']-a)\n    if b>c['due']:ok=False;break\n    a+=c['service']; b+=c['service']; prev=cid\n   if ok:\n    leg=self.dist[prev,0]; driven+=leg; a+=leg*f['pace']; b+=leg*f['pace']\n    latest=min(latest,self.depot['close']-a)\n    earliest=max(self.depot['open'],b-f['shift'])\n    if a>f['shift'] or b>self.depot['close'] or earliest>latest or (f['range'] is not None and driven>f['range']):ok=False\n   z=(f['fixed']+f['per_unit']*driven, earliest) if ok else None\n  self._eval_cache[key]=z; return z\n def total(self,routes): return sum(self.ev(t,s)[0] for t,s in zip(self.slots,routes) if s)\n def options(self,routes,cid,noise=0.0,restrict=None):\n  out=[]\n  inds=range(len(routes)) if restrict is None else restrict\n  for r in inds:\n   typ=self.slots[r]; seq=routes[r]; old=self.ev(typ,seq)[0] if seq else 0\n   for p in range(len(seq)+1):\n    ns=seq[:p]+[cid]+seq[p:]; e=self.ev(typ,ns)\n    if e:\n     delta=e[0]-old\n     score=delta*(1+noise*self.rng.random())\n     out.append((score,delta,r,p))\n  out.sort()\n  return out\n def build(self,randomness=.12):\n  routes=[[] for _ in self.slots]; un=set(self.ids)\n  # Insert by regret-3, strongly prioritizing constrained customers.\n  while un:\n   choices=[]\n   for cid in un:\n    oo=self.options(routes,cid,randomness)\n    if not oo: return None\n    vals=[x[0] for x in oo[:3]]\n    while len(vals)<3: vals.append(vals[-1]+100000)\n    regret=(vals[1]-vals[0])+.35*(vals[2]-vals[0])\n    c=self.C[cid]\n    urgency=150000/(1+(c['due']-c['ready'])) + c['demand']*8\n    choices.append((regret+urgency+self.rng.random()*300, cid, oo))\n   _,cid,oo=max(choices)\n   # usually best; occasional alternate among close options\n   pick=oo[0]\n   if self.rng.random()<randomness and len(oo)>1:\n    lim=min(len(oo),4); pick=oo[self.rng.randrange(lim)]\n   _,_,r,p=pick; routes[r].insert(p,cid); un.remove(cid)\n  return routes\n def reinsert(self,routes,removed,noise=.08):\n  un=set(removed)\n  while un:\n   choices=[]\n   for cid in un:\n    oo=self.options(routes,cid,noise)\n    if not oo:return None\n    vals=[x[0] for x in oo[:3]]\n    while len(vals)<3: vals.append(vals[-1]+100000)\n    regret=(vals[1]-vals[0])+.3*(vals[2]-vals[0])\n    choices.append((regret+self.rng.random()*100,cid,oo))\n   _,cid,oo=max(choices)\n   # randomized restricted candidate list\n   k=1\n   if self.rng.random()<noise and len(oo)>1:k=min(3,len(oo))\n   _,_,r,p=oo[self.rng.randrange(k)]; routes[r].insert(p,cid); un.remove(cid)\n  return routes\n def descent(self,routes,max_round=30):\n  cost=self.total(routes)\n  for rd in range(max_round):\n   improved=False\n   order=list(range(len(routes))); self.rng.shuffle(order)\n   # best relocate per customer, first improvement\n   for a in order:\n    seq=routes[a]\n    for pi in list(range(len(seq))):\n     cid=seq[pi]; src=seq[:pi]+seq[pi+1:]\n     olda=self.ev(self.slots[a],seq)[0]; newa=self.ev(self.slots[a],src)[0] if src else 0\n     inds=list(range(len(routes))); self.rng.shuffle(inds)\n     best=None\n     for b in inds:\n      if b==a: continue\n      oldb=self.ev(self.slots[b],routes[b])[0] if routes[b] else 0\n      for p in range(len(routes[b])+1):\n       dst=routes[b][:p]+[cid]+routes[b][p:]; e=self.ev(self.slots[b],dst)\n       if e:\n        delta=newa+e[0]-olda-oldb\n        if delta<0 and (best is None or delta<best[0]):best=(delta,b,p,src,dst)\n     if best:\n      _,b,p,src,dst=best; routes[a]=src; routes[b]=dst; cost+=best[0]; improved=True; break\n    if improved:break\n   if improved:continue\n   # intra-route 2-opt best\n   best=None\n   for a in order:\n    s=routes[a]; old=self.ev(self.slots[a],s)[0] if s else 0\n    for i in range(len(s)-1):\n     for j in range(i+2,len(s)+1):\n      ns=s[:i]+list(reversed(s[i:j]))+s[j:]; e=self.ev(self.slots[a],ns)\n      if e and e[0]<old and (best is None or e[0]-old<best[0]):best=(e[0]-old,a,ns)\n   if best:\n    cost+=best[0];routes[best[1]]=best[2];continue\n   break\n  return routes\n def search(self,seconds,target):\n  end=time.time()+seconds; best=None; bestc=10**18; starts=0\n  while time.time()<end and bestc>target:\n   r=self.build(.04+.12*self.rng.random()); starts+=1\n   if r is None:continue\n   r=self.descent(r,12); c=self.total(r)\n   if c<bestc:best,bestc=[x[:] for x in r],c;print('initial',bestc,'starts',starts,flush=True)\n   # LNS on this solution\n   cur=[x[:] for x in r]; curc=c; temp=2500\n   for it in range(100000):\n    if time.time()>=end or bestc<=target:break\n    nr=[x[:] for x in cur]\n    non=[i for i,s in enumerate(nr) if s]\n    if not non:break\n    removed=[]\n    mode=self.rng.random()\n    if mode<.22:\n     # whole route removal encourages route elimination\n     rr=self.rng.choice(non); {"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_16","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"cat > /tmp/solve.py <<'PY'\nimport json, math, random, sys, time\nfrom functools import lru_cache\n\nclass Solver:\n def __init__(self,path,seed=1):\n  self.data=json.load(open(path)); self.cs=self.data['customers']; self.n=len(self.cs); self.C={c['id']:c for c in self.cs}; self.ids=list(self.C)\n  self.depot=self.data['depot']; self.types={f['type']:f for f in self.data['fleet']}\n  self.slots=[]\n  for f in self.data['fleet']:\n   self.slots += [f['type']]*f['count']\n  pts={0:self.depot}|self.C\n  self.dist={(a,b): self.distance(pts[a],pts[b]) for a in pts for b in pts}\n  self.rng=random.Random(seed)\n  self._eval_cache={}\n def distance(self,a,b):\n  sq=(a['x']-b['x'])**2+(a['y']-b['y'])**2; d=math.isqrt(sq); return d if d*d==sq else d+1\n def ev(self,typ,seq):\n  if not seq:return (0,self.depot['open'])\n  key=(typ,tuple(seq)); z=self._eval_cache.get(key)\n  if z is not None:return z\n  f=self.types[typ]; cap=sum(self.C[i]['demand'] for i in seq)\n  if cap>f['capacity']: z=None\n  else:\n   driven=0; prev=0; a=0; b=-10**18; latest=10**18\n   ok=True\n   for cid in seq:\n    c=self.C[cid]; leg=self.dist[prev,cid]; driven+=leg; a+=leg*f['pace']; b+=leg*f['pace']\n    latest=min(latest,self.depot['close']-a)\n    earliest=max(self.depot['open'],b-f['shift'])\n    if a>f['shift'] or b>self.depot['close'] or earliest>latest or (f['range'] is not None and driven>f['range']):ok=False\n   z=(f['fixed']+f['per_unit']*driven, earliest) if ok else None\n  self._eval_cache[key]=z; return z\n def total(self,routes): return sum(self.ev(t,s)[0] for t,s in zip(self.slots,routes) if s)\n def options(self,routes,cid,noise=0.0,restrict=None):\n  out=[]\n  inds=range(len(routes)) if restrict is None else restrict\n  for r in inds:\n   typ=self.slots[r]; seq=routes[r]; old=self.ev(typ,seq)[0] if seq else 0\n   for p in range(len(seq)+1):\n    ns=seq[:p]+[cid]+seq[p:]; e=self.ev(typ,ns)\n    if e:\n     delta=e[0]-old\n     score=delta*(1+noise*self.rng.random())\n     out.append((score,delta,r,p))\n  out.sort()\n  return out\n def build(self,randomness=.12):\n  routes=[[] for _ in self.slots]; un=set(self.ids)\n  # Insert by regret-3, strongly prioritizing constrained customers.\n  while un:\n   choices=[]\n   for cid in un:\n    oo=self.options(routes,cid,randomness)\n    if not oo: return None\n    vals=[x[0] for x in oo[:3]]\n    while len(vals)<3: vals.append(vals[-1]+100000)\n    regret=(vals[1]-vals[0])+.35*(vals[2]-vals[0])\n    c=self.C[cid]\n    urgency=150000/(1+(c['due']-c['ready'])) + c['demand']*8\n    choices.append((regret+urgency+self.rng.random()*300, cid, oo))\n   _,cid,oo=max(choices)\n   # usually best; occasional alternate among close options\n   pick=oo[0]\n   if self.rng.random()<randomness and len(oo)>1:\n    lim=min(len(oo),4); pick=oo[self.rng.randrange(lim)]\n   _,_,r,p=pick; routes[r].insert(p,cid); un.remove(cid)\n  return routes\n def reinsert(self,routes,removed,noise=.08):\n  un=set(removed)\n  while un:\n   choices=[]\n   for cid in un:\n    oo=self.options(routes,cid,noise)\n    if not oo:return None\n    vals=[x[0] for x in oo[:3]]\n    while len(vals)<3: vals.append(vals[-1]+100000)\n    regret=(vals[1]-vals[0])+.3*(vals[2]-vals[0])\n    choices.append((regret+self.rng.random()*100,cid,oo))\n   _,cid,oo=max(choices)\n   # randomized restricted candidate list\n   k=1\n   if self.rng.random()<noise and len(oo)>1:k=min(3,len(oo))\n   _,_,r,p=oo[self.rng.randrange(k)]; routes[r].insert(p,cid); un.remove(cid)\n  return routes\n def descent(self,routes,max_round=30):\n  cost=self.total(routes)\n  for rd in range(max_round):\n   improved=False\n   order=list(range(len(routes))); self.rng.shuffle(order)\n   # best relocate per customer, first improvement\n   for a in order:\n    seq=routes[a]\n    for pi in list(range(len(seq))):\n     cid=seq[pi]; src=seq[:pi]+seq[pi+1:]\n     olda=self.ev(self.slots[a],seq)[0]; newa=self.ev(self.slots[a],src)[0] if src else 0\n     inds=list(range(len(routes))); self.rng.shuffle(inds)\n     best=None\n     for b in inds:\n      if b==a: continue\n      oldb=self.ev(self.slots[b],routes[b])[0] if routes[b] else 0\n      for p in range(len(routes[b])+1):\n       dst=routes[b][:p]+[cid]+routes[b][p:]; {"type":"item.started","item":{"id":"item_101","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"cat > /tmp/anchor.py <<'PY'\nimport sys,json,random\nsys.path.insert(0,'/tmp');from solve import Solver\nclass AnchorSolver(Solver):\n def __init__(self,path,seed,k):super().__init__(path,seed);self.k=k\n def build(self,randomness=.12):\n  routes=[[] for _ in self.slots]; trucks=[i for i,t in enumerate(self.slots) if t=='truck'][:self.k]\n  markets=[c['id'] for c in self.cs if c['kind']=='market']\n  # farthest-point anchors, randomized first among high demands\n  first=max(markets,key=lambda x:self.C[x]['demand']+self.rng.random()*10);anchors=[first]\n  while len(anchors)<min(self.k,len(markets)):\n   left=[x for x in markets if x not in anchors]\n   anchors.append(max(left,key=lambda x:min(self.dist[x,a] for a in anchors)*(0.8+0.4*self.rng.random())))\n  for r,cid in zip(trucks,anchors):routes[r]=[cid]\n  un=set(self.ids)-set(anchors); marketleft=set(markets)-set(anchors)\n  # place remaining markets only on active trucks\n  while marketleft:\n   choices=[]\n   for cid in marketleft:\n    oo=self.options(routes,cid,randomness,trucks)\n    if not oo:return None\n    vals=[x[0] for x in oo[:3]]\n    while len(vals)<3:vals.append(vals[-1]+100000)\n    choices.append((vals[1]-vals[0]+self.rng.random()*300,cid,oo))\n   _,cid,oo=max(choices);pick=oo[0] if self.rng.random()>.15 else oo[self.rng.randrange(min(3,len(oo)))]\n   _,_,r,p=pick;routes[r].insert(p,cid);un.remove(cid);marketleft.remove(cid)\n  # standard regret for all others\n  while un:\n   choices=[]\n   for cid in un:\n    oo=self.options(routes,cid,randomness)\n    if not oo:return None\n    vals=[x[0] for x in oo[:3]]\n    while len(vals)<3:vals.append(vals[-1]+100000)\n    c=self.C[cid];reg=(vals[1]-vals[0])+.35*(vals[2]-vals[0])+150000/(1+c['due']-c['ready'])+c['demand']*8\n    choices.append((reg+self.rng.random()*300,cid,oo))\n   _,cid,oo=max(choices);pick=oo[0]\n   if self.rng.random()<randomness and len(oo)>1:pick=oo[self.rng.randrange(min(4,len(oo)))]\n   _,_,r,p=pick;routes[r].insert(p,cid);un.remove(cid)\n  return routes\npath,out,target,secs,seed,k=sys.argv[1],sys.argv[2],int(sys.argv[3]),float(sys.argv[4]),int(sys.argv[5]),int(sys.argv[6])\ns=AnchorSolver(path,seed,k);r,c=s.search(secs,target);print('FINAL',c)\nif r:json.dump(s.plan(r),open(out,'w'),indent=1)\nPY\nfor spec in 'harbour-lanes ha2 77867 2' 'harbour-lanes ha3 77867 3' 'old-town oa5 147362 5' 'old-town oa4 147362 4' 'riverside ra8 201921 8' 'riverside ra7 201921 7' 'north-ring na11 288705 11' 'north-ring na10 288705 10';
